RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = {
	set_variable_to_random = global.RUS_tesla_minesweeper_update
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_open = {
	set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_window_open
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_close = {
	clr_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_window_open
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_start = {
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_reset_board = yes
	set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active
	country_event = { id = RUS_tesla_minesweeper.2 hours = 1 }
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

	random_list = {
		10 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {

				factor = 0
				OR = {

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_finish_victory = {
	clr_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active
	set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_won
	set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_cooldown
	country_event = { id = RUS_tesla_minesweeper.1 days = 60 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_owned_state = {
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				impassable = no
				energy_infrastructure < 1
				industrial_infrastructure < 1
			}
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = ROOT
				impassable = no
				energy_infrastructure < 1
				industrial_infrastructure < 1
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = energy_infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		RUS_tesla_refresh_power_grid_bonuses = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_no_reward
	}
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_finish_defeat = {
	clr_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active
	set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_lost
	set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_cooldown
	country_event = { id = RUS_tesla_minesweeper.1 days = 60 }
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_reveal_00 = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_00 } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_initialised } }
			set_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_first_cell = 0 }
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_generate_board = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_mine_00 }
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_finish_defeat = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_revealed_00
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_count_00 = 0 } }
				RUS_tesla_minesweeper_expand_empty_cells = yes
			}
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_check_victory = yes
		}
	}
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_mark_00 = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_revealed_00 } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_00 }
			clr_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_00
			add_to_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags = -1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags < 10 } }
			set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_00
			add_to_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags = 1 }
		}
	}
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_reveal_01 = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_01 } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_initialised } }
			set_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_first_cell = 1 }
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_generate_board = yes
		}
		if = {

		limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_26 } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_initialised } }
			set_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_first_cell = 26 }
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_generate_board = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_mine_26 }
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_finish_defeat = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_revealed_26
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_count_26 = 0 } }
				RUS_tesla_minesweeper_expand_empty_cells = yes
			}
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_check_victory = yes
		}
	}
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_mark_26 = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_revealed_26 } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_26 }
			clr_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_26
			add_to_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags = -1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags < 10 } }
			set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_26
			add_to_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags = 1 }
		}
	}
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_mine_63 }
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_finish_defeat = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_revealed_63
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_count_63 = 0 } }
				RUS_tesla_minesweeper_expand_empty_cells = yes
			}
			RUS_tesla_minesweeper_check_victory = yes
		}
	}
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}

RUS_tesla_minesweeper_mark_63 = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_active NOT = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_revealed_63 } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_63 }
			clr_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_63
			add_to_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags = -1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags < 10 } }
			set_country_flag = RUS_tesla_minesweeper_marked_63
			add_to_variable = { RUS_tesla_minesweeper_flags = 1 }
		}
	}
	RUS_tesla_minesweeper_refresh_gui = yes
}
